## `AFG-5590-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Dryer-Airflow-Temp Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Dormitory Indoor Air Quality & Emergency Battery Lighting
- **Intro:** Dryer-Airflow-Temp Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Dormitory Indoor Air Quality & Emergency Battery Lighting — System Isolation and Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) Verification: Voltage must be 0 V AC and 0 V DC within ±0.5 V. Lockout devices must be rated for the circuit voltage and current.
- **Key tolerance:** Voltage must be 0 V AC and 0 V DC within ±0.5 V. Lockout devices must be rated for the circuit voltage and current.
- **Standards:** `ISO 7730:2005`, `Standard 62.1-2022`, `IEC 60598-2-22:2022`, `ANSI/UL 924`
